HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B001","root_001124/B003","root_001124/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0a23c436728c7ee81f4d","text":"1. **Seeking an opening becomes a judicial exposure of coercion.** `ف ت ح` `B003` (settling a closed dispute by judgment) and `B008` (inner disclosure) meet `ج ب ر` `B003` (coercive compulsion), `ع ن د` `B001` (resistance to truth), and `خ ي ب` `B001` (missing the sought thing). The later failed plots and fulfilled promise show closure settled against those who tried to compel it. I infer the requested opening functions as adjudication; the coercive resister's failure is the disclosed result. *Istaftahu* retains both victory-seeking and judgment-seeking without collapsing them.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_25ed6cc3f2efeddd9f16","text":"1. **`ٱسْتَفْتَحُوا` retains adjudication and victory as separate openings.** `ف ت ح B003` supplies settling a closed dispute by judgment, while `ف ت ح B004` supplies victory as an opened outcome. The dispute over proof, expulsion, land, and threat in 14:10-14 is followed by failed coercers and then punishment in 14:16-17. The packet supports both roles, so I preserve two readings: they sought a decisive judgment, and they sought an opened victory. The outcome closes neither into the other, though both expose the coercive party's failure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B001","root_001124/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4fc953f18b3b21a4e80c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:15:1\",\"root_001124\",[\"B004\"]],[\"14:15:2\",\"root_000451\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hüsran, zorbanın tam da elde etmek istediği açılıştan yoksun bırakılmasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000451/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_598c9708c3ada69c50e7","text":"3. **Failure is deprivation of the very outcome sought.** `خ ي ب B001` supplies missing the sought thing and being deprived of fortune. Its immediate position after `ٱسْتَفْتَحُوا` makes the requested opening the missing object, while 14:16-18 materialize the opposite outcome. I infer that the syntax turns disappointment into exact reversal. `خَابَ` reads as being shut out from the opening one tried to secure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000216/B003","root_001052/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_676cafcd73f061a52519","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:15:4\",\"root_000216\",[\"B003\"]],[\"14:15:5\",\"root_001052\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Zorlayıcı güç, gerçeğe direnip eğriliği sürdürmenin uygulama aracına dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_771281807716e4537b72","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"14:15:1\",\"root_001124\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İstenen açılış, kapanmış anlaşmazlığı kesin bir hükümle sonuçlandırma talebidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000216/B003","root_001052/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8a7524b80f18de67ab21","text":"2. **The failed figure combines coercion with resistant deviation.** `ج ب ر B003` supplies coercive compulsion, activated by the ultimatum of 14:13; `ع ن د B001` supplies deviation from straightness and resistance to truth, activated by the preceding proof exchange. The packet supplies force and refusal as adjacent functions; I infer that force protects the deviation by trying to eliminate alternatives. `جَبَّارٍ عَنِيد` consequently reads as a mechanism, not two generic insults: coercion operationalizes resistance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9deea1d66e9de8e3e926","text":"14:16-18 materialized the opposite of the opening sought: recursive punishment, blocked passage, and dispersed works.
